# Moved paths (the campaign restructure, 2026-10-18)

The campaign's process records moved out of `audits/` and `briefs/` (and a few top-level files)
into `campaign/`, the maintainer's tools into `devtools/seyoon/`, and the copy-text screen into
`scripts/`, in one commit of pure renames. Records written before then cite the old paths; each
row below gives the new one. `git log --follow <new path>` shows a file's history across the
move.

| Old path | New path |
|---|---|
| `AroraBarakChapter1Plan.md` | `campaign/design/plans/AroraBarakChapter1Plan.md` |
| `AroraBarakChapter2Plan.md` | `campaign/design/plans/AroraBarakChapter2Plan.md` |
